# Agents · H · part 3

957 entries, harness-rubric-steward to healthit-interoperability-engineer. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **harness-rubric-steward**: Validates the harness oracle and project-type standards against current authoritative sources via Tavily MCP. Used exclusively by the critical-harness skill orchestrator. Do NOT activate for any direct user request — this agent is spawned programmatically with oracle, spec, and staging directory paths. ([hashbulla/critical-harness](https://github.com/hashbulla/critical-harness/blob/HEAD/agents/rubric-steward.md))
- **harness-runner**: Drives one agent-harness loop iteration to completion — reads the plan and state files, executes exactly one task with the task skill's own tools, lets the controller verify it, and reports the directive. Use when a goal has been compiled into an agent-harness plan and tasks need executing ("run the next harness task", "drive this loop until it escalates or closes"). Use PROACTIVELY after goal_compiler.py writes a plan. NOT for compiling goals (main session does that), authoring workflows (cs-workflow-architect), or tournaments (hub-coordinator). ([alirezarezvani/claude-skills](https://github.com/alirezarezvani/claude-skills/blob/HEAD/engineering/agent-harness/agents/harness-runner.md) · ★30k · 25 copies)

- **harness-runner**: Executes registered golden packs; emits the five-layer scorecard. Header always carries commit SHA, golden dataset version, model configuration. Annotates PASS-BY-STARVATION wherever an invariant passes on missing upstream data. Runs headless in CI on engine-path PRs and post-deploy against the deployed SHA. Never edits fixtures or thresholds; threshold changes arrive only via owner-signed decisio ([mycrivo/ngoinfo-grantpilot](https://github.com/mycrivo/ngoinfo-grantpilot/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/harness-runner.md))
- **harness-runner**: Measures Aerie Trading's simulation with a headless harness and reports the numbers — multi-seed long-run moments, paired before/after arms, real-engine sensitivities, price-move distributions. Use when a claim about simulation behaviour needs a number, or a run would take minutes or print more than a screen. Give it the question, the arms to compare and the quantity to report. It never changes production code; it reports what it would change. ([quebeco52/aerie-trading](https://github.com/quebeco52/aerie-trading/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/harness-runner.md))
- **harness-runner**: Runs the AI-VIDEO Development Verification Harness at the requested level (focused / related / full) on an exact staged snapshot or commit range, then reports receipt paths, pass/fail per check and failure excerpts. Use after code/doc/config changes when the Parent needs verification evidence; does not edit source, stage files or fix failures. Use after hc-architect has written a cycle document. ([Atharv-110/handicraftui](https://github.com/Atharv-110/handicraftui/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/hc-dev.md) · ★1)
- **hc-distiller**: Distills sessions into delivery memory, runtime learnings, and promotion candidates — supports optional chronicle enrichment; never depends solely on it ([HariharanS/helix](https://github.com/HariharanS/helix/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/hc-distiller.agent.md))
- **hc-explorer**: Workspace-aware context gatherer — searches across repos in the active workspace, spawns sub-explorers, produces file-based context bundles ([HariharanS/helix](https://github.com/HariharanS/helix/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/hc-explorer.agent.md))
- **hc-helix**: Helix coordinator — routes work through phases, manages modes, dispatches to specialist agents ([HariharanS/helix](https://github.com/HariharanS/helix/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/hc-helix.agent.md))
- **hc-implementer**: Implements tasks using TDD — fleet mode runs full red-green-refactor, interactive mode handles green+refactor after tdd-red, manual mode surfaces task contracts for human execution ([HariharanS/helix](https://github.com/HariharanS/helix/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/hc-implementer.agent.md))
- **hc-jam**: Interactive intent refinement — takes a raw feature idea and produces a clear, shared understanding through back-and-forth dialogue ([HariharanS/helix](https://github.com/HariharanS/helix/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/hc-jam.agent.md))
- **hc-planner**: Researches and drafts feature briefs for Handicraft UI on genuinely new capability surfaces — theme builder, docs information architecture, landing page concept, annotation layer, v2 charts. Reads live web for prior art and current best practice, then proposes what to build and why, inside the locked product vision. Deliberately NOT used for routine component builds; the architect works straight from doctrine for those. Answers "what and why", never "how". ([Atharv-110/handicraftui](https://github.com/Atharv-110/handicraftui/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/hc-planner.md) · ★1)
- **hc-planner**: Takes a refined intent and produces a detailed Product Requirements Document (PRD) by exploring domain context and structuring requirements ([HariharanS/helix](https://github.com/HariharanS/helix/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/hc-planner.agent.md))
- **hc-qa**: QA engineer for Handicraft UI. Independently re-runs every gate, writes mutation-verified vitest tests per the architect's QA plan, and verifies in a real browser via chrome-devtools MCP across the 375/768/1280/1920 viewport matrix, both render tiers, both themes, keyboard and axe. Produces severity-ranked findings with reproductions plus a benchmark table, reported to the architect. Adversarial by design. Use after hc-dev returns a manifest. ([Atharv-110/handicraftui](https://github.com/Atharv-110/handicraftui/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/hc-qa.md) · ★1)
- **hc-resume**: Briefs the user on where a feature left off — reads the deterministic resume snapshot, task board, decisions, and (optionally) memory and git log to provide a concise status update ([HariharanS/helix](https://github.com/HariharanS/helix/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/hc-resume.agent.md))
- **hc-reviewer**: Risk and evidence gate — assesses delivery confidence, surfaces evidence gaps, emits deferred verification follow-up tasks, and performs multi-lens semantic review before PR creation ([HariharanS/helix](https://github.com/HariharanS/helix/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/hc-reviewer.agent.md))
- **hc-scribe**: Background state manager — updates task boards, decisions log, and workspace state on behalf of other agents ([HariharanS/helix](https://github.com/HariharanS/helix/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/hc-scribe.agent.md))
- **hc-setup**: Setup owner — validates bootstrap state, resolves workspace inputs, runs script-owned workspace setup, and reports readiness before handoff to Helix ([HariharanS/helix](https://github.com/HariharanS/helix/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/hc-setup.agent.md))
- **hc-tdd-red**: Writes failing tests for a task's acceptance criteria — RED phase of TDD. Does NOT implement production code. ([HariharanS/helix](https://github.com/HariharanS/helix/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/hc-tdd-red.agent.md))
- **hc-ui-tester**: Authors new Playwright tests and debugs failing ones. NOT invoked for green-path test runs. ([HariharanS/helix](https://github.com/HariharanS/helix/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/hc-ui-tester.agent.md))
- **hc-writer**: Content and technical writer for Handicraft UI. Owns every word that ships — docs pages, landing copy, component descriptions, API reference prose, error and empty states, down to single headings. Holds every piece it writes to VOICE.md, and drafts amendments to VOICE.md rather than writing that file itself. Its output is always shipped prose, never the internal register. - **hdir**: **Purpose**: Defines the vendor-agnostic Hardware Design Intermediate Representation (HDIR) — the shared data shape that all EDA parsers (KiCad/Altium/OrCAD/etc.) emit and all downstream hardware analyzers consume. This is the minimal core needed by the HD.2 PCB SI analyzer; broader ingestion coverage arrives with HD.1 parsers. ([limit5/OmniSight-Productizer](https://github.com/limit5/OmniSight-Productizer/blob/HEAD/docs/architecture/agents/hdir.md))
- **hdr**: Handles HDR color workflows end-to-end — HDR10 static metadata, HDR10+ dynamic metadata, Dolby Vision profiles, PQ↔HLG conversion, SDR↔HDR tone mapping, and ACES/OCIO color management. Use when the user mentions Dolby Vision, HDR10, HDR10+, HLG, PQ, tone mapping, or anything about color pipelines that must survive delivery. ([damionrashford/media-os](https://github.com/damionrashford/media-os/blob/HEAD/agents/hdr.md) · ★25)
- **hdr-benchmark-runner**: Run HDR conformance probes and benchmarks on a live session. Use to collect EDID, DRM, Wayland protocol, compositor, and capture evidence for Hyprland, KWin, or another stack, then produce a JSON and Markdown report. Read-only with respect to system configuration. ([SimpleSoftwareLTDA/hdr-conformance](https://github.com/SimpleSoftwareLTDA/hdr-conformance/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/hdr-benchmark-runner.md))
- **hdr-spec-reviewer**: Review HDR changes against the hdr-conformance rule set. Use for pull requests or patches that touch EDID parsing, Wayland color management, DRM HDR metadata, tone mapping, HLG, ICC, or HDR capture. Produces evidence-backed findings with rule IDs, severity, file and line references, and a concrete fix. Read-only. ([SimpleSoftwareLTDA/hdr-conformance](https://github.com/SimpleSoftwareLTDA/hdr-conformance/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/hdr-spec-reviewer.md))
- **hdr-standard-editor**: Maintain the normative HDR conformance rule set, profiles, and versioning for the hdr-conformance project. Use when adding, changing, or deprecating a rule, profile, or expected value, and when checking that every rule has a test and every test maps to a rule. Keeps the specification internally consistent. ([SimpleSoftwareLTDA/hdr-conformance](https://github.com/SimpleSoftwareLTDA/hdr-conformance/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/hdr-standard-editor.md))
- **hdr-test-author**: Write HDR conformance tests, fixtures, and golden vectors for the hdr-conformance suite. Use when adding a scenario, EDID fixture, protocol test, pipeline vector, or capture check. Produces deterministic tests that map to a rule ID and run in CI without HDR hardware when possible. ([SimpleSoftwareLTDA/hdr-conformance](https://github.com/SimpleSoftwareLTDA/hdr-conformance/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/hdr-test-author.md))
- **HDS_CONSTITUTION_AGENT_PROMPT**: &gt; **Instructions for Product & Engineering**: Copy and paste the system prompt below into your AI tool of choice (e.g., **Claude Projects**, **ChatGPT Custom GPT**, **Cursor System Instructions**, **GitHub Copilot**, or **Anthropic API**). ([Lenindesign/hearst](https://github.com/Lenindesign/hearst/blob/HEAD/agents/HDS_CONSTITUTION_AGENT_PROMPT.md))
- **he-2026-agent-lightning-v1-0-towards-harnessed**: Agent Lightning v1.0은 배포할 때 쓰는 agent harness가 agent loop를 그대로 소유한 채 강화학습을 진행하는 방식을 harnessed agentic RL로 명명하고, 그 구도에서만 생기는 네 가지 공학 문제를 정리한 Microsoft 중심 연구다. 논문은 retokenization, advantage 계산, loss normalization, 학습 백엔드 스케줄링을 문제로 지목하고, 각각에 대한 자기 선택을 약 3,500줄짜리 프레임워크에 구현해 세 가지 agent 설정에서 검증했다. ([sguys99/ai-wiki](https://github.com/sguys99/ai-wiki/blob/HEAD/wiki/agents/he-2026-agent-lightning-v1-0-towards-harnessed.md))

- **health-verifier**: Verifies running services are actually healthy — checks container `(healthy)` status, custom Prometheus metrics presence, AMQP consumer attachment, and queue depth steady-state. Emits HEALTH_REPORT.md. Use post-deploy, during /osEngineer:verify, or when a tracer-bullet needs a liveness baseline. Don't use as a substitute for the verifier agent (the verifier owns PHASE_PLAN acceptance criteria); don't use for static scans (route to red-team-local). ([andreas2301/safeanalyse](https://github.com/andreas2301/safeanalyse/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/health-verifier.md))
- **health-watchdog-mcp-maintainer**: Fix issues, add features, and maintain quality in health-watchdog-mcp. MCP server that monitors system health — CPU, memory, disk, Chrome/Node processes, Docker, and network — via shell commands. Use PROACTIVELY when modifying this repo. MUST read DECISIONS.md before any code change. ([kurzawsl/health-watchdog-mcp](https://github.com/kurzawsl/health-watchdog-mcp/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/health-watchdog-mcp-maintainer.md))
- **health-watcher**: Scheduled agent that tracks health score movements across the book of business and surfaces meaningful changes before they become visible problems. Detects directional movement — drops, recoveries, sudden changes — and routes only accounts with meaningful change to the alert output. Stable accounts are logged but not surfaced. Trigger phrases: "run health watcher", "check health scores", "health score alerts", or on schedule. Config at `~/.claude/plugins/config/claude-for-customer-success/csm/CLAUDE.md`.
